## Activities

### create

`activities.create`

Create a calendar activity; attendees get an email invitation when calendar sync is on

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.pipedrive.api.activities.create({ subject: 'Follow up', type: 'call' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `subject` | `string` | No | — |
| `type` | `string` | No | — |
| `owner_id` | `number` | No | — |
| `deal_id` | `number` | No | — |
| `lead_id` | `string` | No | — |
| `org_id` | `number` | No | — |
| `project_id` | `number` | No | — |
| `due_date` | `string` | No | — |
| `due_time` | `string` | No | — |
| `duration` | `string` | No | — |
| `busy` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `done` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `location` | `object` | No | — |
| `participants` | `object[]` | No | — |
| `attendees` | `object[]` | No | — |
| `public_description` | `string` | No | — |
| `priority` | `number` | No | — |
| `outcome` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `note` | `string` | No | — |

OAuth requires activities:full. All request properties are optional in the v2 schema. Use participants to set the person; the response data is an activity object. When the owner has calendar sync on, Pipedrive emails an invitation to every attendee, so this operation needs approval. The documented API token cost is 5 tokens.

```ts
  value?: string,
```

### delete

`activities.delete`

Mark an activity deleted

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.pipedrive.api.activities.delete({ id: 1 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number` | Yes | — |

OAuth requires activities:full. Deletion marks an activity deleted; Pipedrive permanently deletes it after 30 days. The documented API token cost is 3 tokens.



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  id?: number | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### get

`activities.get`

Get an activity by its numeric ID

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.pipedrive.api.activities.get({ id: 1 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `include_fields` | `attendees[]` | No | — |

OAuth requires activities:read or activities:full. include_fields currently accepts attendees. To associate a person on create/update, use participants with primary: true; person_id is not an accepted body field. The documented API token cost is 1 token.



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>

### list

`activities.list`

List activities with cursor pagination and filters

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.pipedrive.api.activities.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `filter_id` | `number` | No | — |
| `ids` | `number[]` | No | — |
| `owner_id` | `number` | No | — |
| `deal_id` | `number` | No | — |
| `lead_id` | `string` | No | — |
| `person_id` | `number` | No | — |
| `org_id` | `number` | No | — |
| `done` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `updated_since` | `string` | No | — |
| `updated_until` | `string` | No | — |
| `sort_by` | `id \| update_time \| add_time \| due_date` | No | — |
| `sort_direction` | `asc \| desc` | No | — |
| `include_fields` | `attendees[]` | No | — |
| `limit` | `number` | No | — |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | — |

OAuth requires activities:read or activities:full. The limit is 1–500; use the opaque additional_data.next_cursor value as cursor. Array filters are comma-separated. The documented API token cost is 10 tokens.



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `data` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `additional_data` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  id?: number | null,
  subject?: string | null,
  type?: string | null,
  owner_id?: number | null,
  creator_user_id?: number | null,
  is_deleted?: boolean | null,
  add_time?: string | null,
  update_time?: string | null,
  deal_id?: number | null,
```

### update

`activities.update`

Update a calendar activity; attendees get updated invitations when calendar sync is on

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.pipedrive.api.activities.update({ id: 1, done: true });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `subject` | `string` | No | — |
| `type` | `string` | No | — |
| `owner_id` | `number` | No | — |
| `deal_id` | `number` | No | — |
| `lead_id` | `string` | No | — |
| `org_id` | `number` | No | — |
| `project_id` | `number` | No | — |
| `due_date` | `string` | No | — |
| `due_time` | `string` | No | — |
| `duration` | `string` | No | — |
| `busy` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `done` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `location` | `object` | No | — |
| `participants` | `object[]` | No | — |
| `attendees` | `object[]` | No | — |
| `public_description` | `string` | No | — |
| `priority` | `number` | No | — |
| `outcome` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `note` | `string` | No | — |

OAuth requires activities:full. The path ID is supplied as the input id and omitted from the JSON body. With calendar sync on, changed guests, time or place email updated invitations or cancellations to attendees. The documented API token cost is 5 tokens.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="location full type">

```ts

  value?: string,
  country?: string,
  admin_area_level_1?: string,
  admin_area_level_2?: string,

  route?: string,
  street_number?: string,
  subpremise?: string,
  postal_code?: string
}
```

```ts
  primary?: boolean
}[]
```

## Api V1

### activityFields.getActivityFields

`apiV1.activityFields.getActivityFields`

Get all activity fields (GET /activityFields; 20 API tokens; OAuth scopes: activities:read, activities:full)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.pipedrive.api.apiV1.activityFields.getActivityFields({  });
```

**Input:** _empty object_

GET https://api.pipedrive.com/v1/activityFields. The official OpenAPI x-token-cost is 20 API tokens. OAuth scope(s): activities:read, activities:full. See https://developers.pipedrive.com/docs/api/v1/ActivityFields. API tokens use x-api-token; OAuth access tokens use Bearer authorization.


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `data` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `additional_data` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  id?: number | null | null,
  key?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  order_nr?: number | null,
  field_type?: boolean | string | double | string | int | string | json | string | date | string | daterange | string | time | string | timerange | string | text | string | varchar | string | varchar_auto | string | varchar_options | string | address | string | enum | string | monetary | string | phone | string | set | string | activity | string | deal | string | lead | string | org | string | people | string | pipeline | string | product | string | project | string | stage | string | user | string | billing_frequency | string | picture | string | price_list | string | projects_board | string | projects_phase | string | status | string | visible_to | string | null,
  add_time?: string | null,
  update_time?: string | null | null,
  last_updated_by_user_id?: number | null | null,
  created_by_user_id?: number | null | null,
  active_flag?: boolean | null,
  edit_flag?: boolean | null,
  index_visible_flag?: boolean | null,
  details_visible_flag?: boolean | null,
  add_visible_flag?: boolean | null,
  important_flag?: boolean | null,
  bulk_edit_allowed?: boolean | null,
  searchable_flag?: boolean | null,
  filtering_allowed?: boolean | null,
  sortable_flag?: boolean | null,
  mandatory_flag?: boolean | Record<string, any> | null,
  options?: {
  }[] | null | null,
  options_deleted?: {
  }[] | null,
  is_subfield?: boolean | null,
  subfields?: {
  }[] | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="additional_data full type">

```ts
{
  start?: number | null,
  limit?: number | null,
  more_items_in_collection?: boolean | null
} | null
```

### activityTypes.addActivityType

`apiV1.activityTypes.addActivityType`

Add new activity type (POST /activityTypes; 10 API tokens; OAuth scopes: admin)

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.pipedrive.api.apiV1.activityTypes.addActivityType({ body: { "name": "x", "icon_key": "task" } });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

POST https://api.pipedrive.com/v1/activityTypes. The official OpenAPI x-token-cost is 10 API tokens. OAuth scope(s): admin. See https://developers.pipedrive.com/docs/api/v1/ActivityTypes. API tokens use x-api-token; OAuth access tokens use Bearer authorization.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  name: string,
  icon_key: task | email | meeting | deadline | call | lunch | calendar | downarrow | document | smartphone | camera | scissors | cogs | bubble | uparrow | checkbox | signpost | shuffle | addressbook | linegraph | picture | car | world | search | clip | sound | brush | key | padlock | pricetag | suitcase | finish | plane | loop | wifi | truck | cart | bulb | bell | presentation,
  color?: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  id?: number | null,
  name?: string | null,
  icon_key?: task | string | email | string | meeting | string | deadline | string | call | string | lunch | string | calendar | string | downarrow | string | document | string | smartphone | string | camera | string | scissors | string | cogs | string | bubble | string | uparrow | string | checkbox | string | signpost | string | shuffle | string | addressbook | string | linegraph | string | picture | string | car | string | world | string | search | string | clip | string | sound | string | brush | string | key | string | padlock | string | pricetag | string | suitcase | string | finish | string | plane | string | loop | string | wifi | string | truck | string | cart | string | bulb | string | bell | string | presentation | string | null,
  color?: string | null,
  order_nr?: number | null,
  key_string?: string | null,
  active_flag?: boolean | null,
  is_custom_flag?: boolean | null,
  add_time?: string | null,
```

### goals.addGoal

`apiV1.goals.addGoal`

Add a new goal (POST /goals; 10 API tokens; OAuth scopes: goals:full)

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.pipedrive.api.apiV1.goals.addGoal({ body: { "title": "Quarterly target", "assignee": { "id": 1, "type": "person" }, "type": { "name": "deals_won", "params": { "pipeline_id": [1] } }, "expected_outcome": { "target": 10, "tracking_metric": "quantity" }, "duration": { "start": "2026-01-01", "end": "2026-03-31" }, "interval": "quarterly" } });
```

|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `body` | `object` | Yes | — |

POST https://api.pipedrive.com/v1/goals. The official OpenAPI x-token-cost is 10 API tokens. OAuth scope(s): goals:full. See https://developers.pipedrive.com/docs/api/v1/Goals. API tokens use x-api-token; OAuth access tokens use Bearer authorization. The strict nested body follows the official examples: assignee has numeric id and person/company/team type; type.name is a documented goal type and params accepts pipeline_id/activity_type_id arrays or null and stage_id; expected_outcome has numeric target, quantity/sum metric, and optional currency_id; duration has start and nullable end.

```ts
    }
  },

    target: number,
    tracking_metric: quantity | sum,
    currency_id?: number
  },
  duration: {
    start: string,
    end: string | null
  },
  interval: weekly | monthly | quarterly | yearly
}
```

```ts
  im?: {
    value?: string | null,
    primary?: boolean | null,
    label?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  birthday?: string | null,
  job_title?: string | null,
  custom_fields?: Record<string, any> | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Products

### create

`products.create`

Create a product

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.pipedrive.api.products.create({ name: 'Sample product', prices: [{ price: 20, currency: 'USD' }] });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `name` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `code` | `string` | No | — |
| `description` | `string` | No | — |
| `unit` | `string` | No | — |
| `tax` | `number` | No | — |
| `category` | `number` | No | — |
| `owner_id` | `number` | No | — |
| `is_linkable` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `visible_to` | `1 \| 3 \| 5 \| 7` | No | — |
| `prices` | `object[]` | No | — |
| `custom_fields` | `Record<string, any>` | No | — |
| `billing_frequency` | `one-time \| annually \| semi-annually \| quarterly \| monthly \| weekly` | No | — |
| `billing_frequency_cycles` | `number \| null` | No | — |

OAuth requires products:full. name is required and cannot be empty; prices require both price and currency. billing_frequency and billing_frequency_cycles are available on Growth and above, and cycles are limited to 1–208 when non-null. The documented API token cost is 5 tokens.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="prices full type">

```ts
{
  price: number,
  currency: string,
  cost?: number,
  direct_cost?: number,
  notes?: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  id?: number | null,
  name?: string | null,
  code?: string | null,
  unit?: string | null,
  tax?: number | null,
  is_deleted?: boolean | null,
  is_linkable?: boolean | null,
  visible_to?: 1 | 3 | 5 | 7 | null,
  owner_id?: number | null,
  add_time?: string | null,
  update_time?: string | null,
  description?: string | null,
  category?: string | null,
  custom_fields?: Record<string, any> | null,
  billing_frequency?: one-time | annually | semi-annually | quarterly | monthly | weekly | null,
  billing_frequency_cycles?: number | null | null,
  prices?: {
    price?: number | null,
    cost?: number | null,
    product_id?: number | null,
    product_variation_id?: number | null,
    currency?: string | null,
    direct_cost?: number | null,
    notes?: string | null
  }[] | null
} | null
```

### images.update

`products.images.update`

Replace a product image with binary data. Takes the file as a public link, base64 or a Blob; on Corsair, pass a Files id.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.pipedrive.api.products.images.update({ id: 1, data: new Blob([await file.arrayBuffer()], { type: file.type }) });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `data` | `string \| object \| custom` | Yes | The image (GIF, JPG, PNG or WEBP): a public URL, &#123;url, filename?, contentType?&#125;, or &#123;base64, filename?, contentType?&#125;; a Blob or File from TypeScript. On Corsair, pass a Files id (fil_…). |
| `file_name` | `string` | No | — |

OAuth requires products:full. In this example, file is an existing runtime File containing the image; its actual bytes are wrapped in a Blob for the required multipart/form-data binary field data. Pass real image bytes. Pipedrive needs a file name with an extension: pass file_name, or the plugin names it `image.<type>` from the Blob type (a name without an extension saves an image Pipedrive then fails to read). The documented API token cost is 20 tokens.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
string | {
  url: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | {
  base64: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | custom
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `data` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  id?: number | null,
  product_id?: number | null,
  company_id?: string | null,
  public_url?: string | null,
  add_time?: string | null,
  mime_type?: string | null,
  name?: string | null
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### search

`products.search`

Search products by term

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.pipedrive.api.products.search({ term: 'sample' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `term` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `fields` | `(code \| custom_fields \| name)[]` | No | — |
| `exact_match` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `include_fields` | `product.price[]` | No | — |
| `limit` | `number` | No | — |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | — |

OAuth accepts products:read, products:full, or search:read. term is 2–100 characters, or one character with exact_match: true; the limit is at most 100 and pagination uses next_cursor. Search has a separate burst limit of 10 requests per 2-second window across plans and authentication types. The documented API token cost is 20 tokens.



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `additional_data` | `object \| null` | No | — |

```ts
      code?: string | null,
      visible_to?: number | null,
      owner?: {
        id?: number | null

} | null
```

## Tasks

### create

`tasks.create`

Create a task in a project

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.pipedrive.api.tasks.create({ title: 'Review proposal', project_id: 1 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `title` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `project_id` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `parent_task_id` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `done` | `0 \| 1` | No | — |
| `milestone` | `0 \| 1` | No | — |
| `due_date` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `start_date` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `assignee_id` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `assignee_ids` | `number[]` | No | — |
| `priority` | `number \| null` | No | — |

OAuth requires projects:full. The Tasks endpoints are marked beta by Pipedrive. title and project_id are required; done and milestone use 0/1 integers, and assignee_ids accepts up to 10 IDs. The documented API token cost is 5 tokens.



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `data` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `additional_data` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
  creator_id?: number | null,
  description?: string | null,
  project_id?: number | null,
  is_done?: boolean | null,
  is_milestone?: boolean | null,
  due_date?: string | null,
  start_date?: string | null,
  parent_task_id?: number | null,
  assignee_ids?: number[] | null,
  priority?: number | null,
  add_time?: string | null,
  update_time?: string | null,
  marked_as_done_time?: string | null
} | null
```

```ts
} | null
```
